*Gene Tangtartharakul,Katherine R. Storrs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过视觉神经科学工具分析了视觉MoE模型中专家（expert）的特化机制，发现专家特化远超类别路由层面，专家对持续的视觉和语义维度（如动-物非动物区分）具有广泛调谐，且这种调谐在跨随机初始化时保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE解释多聚焦于路由分析，但路由本身无法揭示专家实际编码的内容。作者希望超越类别路由，从神经科学视角深入理解专家特化的本质。

Method: 在自然图像上训练基于对比学习的稀疏门控卷积MoE模型，借助视觉神经科学工具（最兴奋输入、类别可分性度量、THINGS语义维度、表征相似性分析）对专家进行门控级和专家级分析。

Result: 1) 动-物非动物区分主导了专家划分（从门控到读出层均一致）；2) 专家调谐是连续的、跨类别边界的视觉与语义维度；3) 专家间类别可分性相似但特征调谐不同；4) 专家特化分配在跨随机初始化时具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 视觉MoE的专家特化远不止类别路由，需通过细粒度的专家级调谐和表征结构分析才能更好理解。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are often interpreted by analysing which categories are routed to which experts. However, routing alone does not reveal what each expert actually encodes. We train sparsely-gated convolutional MoE models with a contrastive objective on natural images and characterise expert specialisation using tools from visual neuroscience. Extending from gating-level to expert-level analyses, we measure per-expert category separability, and per-expert tuning using the most exciting inputs. Extending from category-level to feature-level explanations, we interpret tuning via semantic dimensions derived from a dataset of human behavioural judgements (THINGS). Finally, we use tuning and representational similarity analysis to assess the stability of expertise-allocation across independent initialisations. We find that an animate-inanimate distinction dominates expert partitioning, apparent from gating through to expert readout, and is stable across independently trained models. Although routing statistics suggest relatively sparse, categorical preferences, expert analyses reveal broader tuning to continuous visual and semantic dimensions that extend beyond category boundaries. Experts exhibit similar category-separability to one another, despite distinct feature tuning, demonstrating the explanatory benefits of moving beyond category-level analyses. Together, these results show that expert specialisation in vision MoEs extends well beyond category routing and is better understood by probing fine-grained expert-level tuning and representational structure.

</details>

*Taesung Kwon,Jonghyun Park,Hyungjin Chung,Jong Chul Ye*

TL;DR: 提出AVIS框架，利用自回归视频扩散模型实现流式视频恢复，将初始延迟从114秒降至4秒，吞吐量从0.71 FPS提升至1.18 FPS；进一步提出AVIS Flash变体，仅对第一个块强制执行测量一致性，在单张RTX 4090上将吞吐量提升至5.91 FPS，为实时部署铺平道路。

Method: 提出AVIS框架，核心思想是利用自回归视频扩散模型以流式方式恢复视频，消除延迟瓶颈；通过测量一致性估计初始化反向扩散，减少所需采样步数；AVIS Flash变体进一步仅在第一个块强制执行测量一致性以大幅提升吞吐量。

Result: 相比领先的非自回归求解器，AVIS将初始延迟从114秒降至4秒，吞吐量从0.71 FPS提升至1.18 FPS，并实现更优的恢复质量；AVIS Flash在单张RTX 4090上达到5.91 FPS吞吐量，同时保持竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: AVIS框架有效解决了视频逆问题中实时部署的延迟和吞吐量瓶颈，AVIS Flash在效率与性能之间取得了有利的权衡，为实时应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide powerful priors for zero-shot video inverse problems, but their real-time deployment is hindered by two inefficiencies: high initial latency caused by holistic video restoration, and low throughput resulting from multiple VAE passes to enforce measurement consistency in pixel space. To overcome these limitations, we propose Autoregressive Video Inverse problem Solver (AVIS). The AVIS framework leverages autoregressive video diffusion models to restore videos in a streaming manner, naturally eliminating latency bottlenecks. Specifically, AVIS initializes reverse diffusion with a measurement-consistent estimate, reducing the required sampling steps. Compared to leading non-autoregressive solvers, AVIS drastically reduces initial latency from 114s to 4s and increases throughput from 0.71 to 1.18 FPS while achieving superior restoration quality. We further introduce a highly accelerated variant, dubbed AVIS Flash, that enforces measurement consistency solely on the first chunk. AVIS Flash substantially boosts throughput to 5.91 FPS on a single RTX 4090 GPU while maintaining competitive performance and achieving a favorable efficiency-performance trade-off, paving the way toward real-time deployment.

</details>

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models often face a severe trilemma in human portrait generation: text-image alignment, photorealism, and human-perceived aesthetics inherently inhibit one another. Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is an effective method for enhancing the photorealism of image generation. However, it often leads to overfitting to the training dataset, corrupts pre-trained image priors, and degrades alignment or aesthetics. To break this bottleneck, we propose a feature supervision paradigm for Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiT). Specifically, we introduce a lightweight cross-modal alignment mechanism that implicitly extracts multi-granularity vision-aligned text representations from SigLIP 2 and applies supervision to the image branch of MM-DiT during the training stage, with zero extra inference overhead. Our method injects vision-aligned text guidance while preserving the base model's original generalization, avoiding degradation caused by SFT. Furthermore, our method directly mines implicit multi-granularity aesthetic signals from pre-trained vision foundation models to optimize human-perceived aesthetics. Extensive experiments on MM-DiTs show that our method pushes the Pareto frontier and achieves synergistic improvements across text-image alignment, photorealism, and human-perceived aesthetics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Foggy conditions are commonly encountered in real-world applications; however, existing action recognition approaches typically assume favorable weather and high-quality video inputs. On foggy days, unpredictable visibility degradation and reduced contrast obstruct the extraction of semantic cues, posing significant challenges for current action recognition methods. In this paper, we mitigate the issues faced in action recognition under foggy conditions by employing two strategies. First, we present FogAct, the first benchmark dataset for foggy action recognition, consisting of paired clean and foggy videos captured with a stereo camera system. The dataset spans 10 scenes and 55 action categories, comprising nearly 10,000 video clips. Second, we propose FogNet, a two-stream CLIP model that discovers fog-invariant semantic information hidden behind the degraded videos. FogNet learns robust representations of foggy videos with guidance from clean videos, effectively capturing shared structural and motion cues between clean and foggy videos. Extensive experiments on FogAct and three other popular datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. Our FogAct and FogNet are given in our project page.

</details>

Abstract: Existing deep learning frameworks for Optical Coherence Tomography Angiography (OCTA) vessel segmentation are largely derived from the U-Net architecture, which serves as the foundation for most current designs. However, most of these methods focus only on holistic representation, struggling to address the problem of low local contrast unique to OCTA, which leads to vessel discontinuities and loss of detail. To address these problems, we propose LSENet, which builds upon the U-Net architecture by introducing three core innovative modules: To address vessel discontinuities, we introduce the Patch Information Enhance module (PIE), which replaces standard skip connections to execute patch-wise attention. To mitigate detail loss, the Multiscale Feature Fusion module (MFF) is proposed to feed the PIE module rich, multi-scale information by extracting visually interpretable features from both the original input and preceding layers. Finally, the Connectivity Refinement Decoder (CRD) is designed to refine features from all levels and utilize a large kernel in the final convolutional layer to reduce fragmentation. Experiments on three public datasets (OCTA-500, ROSE-1, and ROSSA) demonstrate that our proposed LSENet achieves state-of-the-art performance while requiring fewer parameters.

</details>


### [45] [RoPeSLR: 3D RoPE-driven Sparse-LowRank Attention for Efficient Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20659)
*Yuxi Liu,Zekun Zhang,Yixiang Cai,Renjia Deng,Yutong He,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoPeSLR框架，通过3D RoPE引导的稀疏-低秩注意力机制，解决了扩散Transformer在长序列视频生成中由于标准线性注意力无法保持旋转位置编码结构而导致的性能退化问题，在90%稀疏度下实现高达10倍FLOPs降低和2.26倍推理加速，且生成保真度损失低于1.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiTs）在高保真视频生成中表现出色，但其二次复杂度（O(L²)）成为长序列合成的瓶颈。现有的稀疏-线性注意力混合方法在极端稀疏度下性能严重下降，根本原因在于"RoPE困境"：标准线性注意力无法保留3D旋转位置编码（RoPE）的正交相对位置结构，从而丧失了关键的距离感知能力。

Method: 提出RoPeSLR框架，包含三个核心要素：(1) 理论上证明DiT注意力流形可解耦为高频语义尖峰集（O(L^{3/2})稀疏度有界）和极端低秩背景连续体（O(d_h log L)）；(2) 基于该结构先验，放弃标准线性注意力，采用头级低秩参数化并配备可学习的3D绝对位置编码注入；(3) 通过保证次二次稀疏度和次线性秩增长，实现超长视频推理的高效扩展。

Result: 在90%稀疏度下，RoPeSLR在Wan2.1-1.3B上实现高达10倍FLOPs减少，在HunyuanVideo-13B的10万+token超长序列上实现2.26倍端到端推理加速，同时保持近无损的生成保真度（平均VBench退化低于1.3%）。

Conclusion: RoPeSLR通过3D RoPE引导的稀疏-低秩注意力框架，有效解决了扩散Transformer在长序列视频生成中的计算瓶颈，在保持生成质量的前提下实现了显著的效率和可扩展性提升。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have revolutionized high-fidelity video generation, yet their $\mathcal{O}(L^2)$ attention complexity poses a formidable bottleneck for long-sequence synthesis. While recent sparse-linear attention hybrids aim to mitigate this, their performance severely degrades at extreme sparsity due to the "RoPE Dilemma": standard linear attention fails to preserve the orthogonal relative-position structure of 3D Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE), neutralizing vital distance awareness. To address this, we propose \textbf{RoPeSLR}, a 3D RoPE-guided Sparse-LowRank attention framework. We establish that under empirically validated assumptions, the DiT attention manifold admits a decoupling into a high-frequency semantic spike set (bounded by $\mathcal{O}(L^{3/2})$ sparsity) and an extreme low-rank ($\mathcal{O}(d_h \log L)$) background continuum. Guided by this structural prior, RoPeSLR eschews standard linear attention for a head-wise low-rank parameterization equipped with a learnable 3D Absolute Positional Embedding (PE) injection, seamlessly synthesizing long-range relative distance decay. By guaranteeing sub-quadratic sparsity and sub-linear rank growth, RoPeSLR is exceptionally suited for scaling to ultra-long video inference. Extensive evaluations validate this scalable superiority: at 90\% sparsity, RoPeSLR achieves up to $10\times$ fewer FLOPs on Wan2.1-1.3B and delivers a $2.26\times$ end-to-end inference speedup on the ultra-long 100K+ token sequences of HunyuanVideo-13B, all while maintaining near-lossless generation fidelity (less than 1.3\% average VBench degradation).

</details>

*Liming Hou,Yueping Peng,Hexiang Hao,Ji Wang,Xuekai Zhang,Wei Tang,Zecong Ye,Xin Ying,Yubo He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对RGB-红外遥感图像对中无人机小目标检测面临的目标尺度小、背景杂乱及异构传感器空间未对齐等问题，提出LER-YOLO框架，通过不确定性感知的目标对齐模块和可靠性引导的稀疏MoE融合模块，实现可靠的跨模态交互，在MBU基准上达到89.7±0.2% AP50。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出LER-YOLO框架：1）不确定性感知目标对齐模块（UTA），将可见光特征重采样至红外参考并估计空间可靠性图；2）可靠性引导的稀疏MoE融合模块（RG-MoE），根据可靠性先验从RGB主导、红外主导和交互融合专家中自适应选择k个专家，实现可信跨模态交互。

Result: 在MBU基准、YOLOv5s-family协议下，三次独立实验平均AP50为89.7±0.2%，最佳为89.9%。消融、参数匹配对比、合成偏移评估及复杂度分析证实增益主要来自可靠性引导的专家路由而非模型容量增加。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨模态对齐可靠性并指导稀疏专家融合，有效抑制不可靠融合，提升未对齐RGB-红外场景下的小目标无人机检测性能。

Abstract: Detecting small unmanned aerial vehicles from RGB-infrared remote-sensing pairs remains challenging due to tiny target scale, cluttered backgrounds, and spatial misalignment between heterogeneous sensors. Existing bimodal detectors often align or fuse features without assessing the reliability of local cross-sensor correspondence, allowing mismatch artifacts to propagate into the detection head. To address this issue, we propose LER-YOLO, a reliability-aware sparse mixture-of-experts framework for misaligned RGB-infrared UAV detection. LER-YOLO first introduces an Uncertainty-Aware Target Alignment module that resamples visible features toward the infrared reference and estimates a spatial reliability map. This reliability prior is then used by a Reliability-Guided Sparse MoE Fusion module to adaptively select k experts from RGB-dominant, infrared-dominant, and interactive fusion experts, enabling trustworthy cross-modal interaction while suppressing unreliable fusion. Experiments on the public MBU benchmark under a YOLOv5s-family protocol show that LER-YOLO achieves 89.7+/-0.2% AP50 over three independent seeds, with a best result of 89.9%. Extensive ablations, parameter-matched comparisons, synthetic-shift evaluations, and complexity analysis demonstrate that the gains mainly come from reliability-guided expert routing rather than increased model capacity.

</details>

Abstract: Establishing a clear link between model predictions and the visual evidence that supports them is critical for transparency and reliability in multimodal reasoning, yet current multimodal large language model (MLLM) evaluations do not explicitly enforce this alignment. Existing benchmarks assess either textual answer correctness or pixel-level localization in isolation, leaving the coupling of reasoning and grounding an open challenge. We introduce VISTAQA, a comprehensive benchmark for joint evaluation of free-form answer correctness and pixel-level evidence grounding in visual question answering. VISTAQA comprises 1,157 expert-curated samples spanning six task types and six visual domains, ranging from direct perception to compositional and relational reasoning. VISTAQA requires models to not only answer correctly, but to also provide precise segmentation masks that support their answers. It also includes hallucination-aware examples where no valid visual evidence exists. To support this enhanced evaluation, we introduce GROVE, a unified evaluation metric that enforces joint correctness by combining textual accuracy and grounding quality via a per-sample geometric mean, ensuring neither dimension can compensate for deficiencies in the other. Comprehensive experiments across grounding-aware models and hybrid pipelines with general-purpose MLLMs reveal that even the strongest systems achieve limited performance under GROVE, highlighting a substantial gap between answer accuracy and visual evidence alignment.

</details>


### [48] [DarkShake-DVS: Event-based Human Action Recognition under Low-light andShaking Camera Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20680)
*Jiaqi Chen,Qinfu Xu,Liyuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EIS-HAR 提出了一种结合事件相机和 IMU 的人体动作识别方法，通过非线性运动补偿模块和混合架构特征提取模块，在低光照和六自由度运动条件下取得 SOTA 结果，并发布了大规模基准数据集 DarkShake-DVS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HAR 方法在低光照和六自由度相机运动条件下性能严重退化；当前缺乏同时包含低光照、六自由度运动和同步 IMU 数据的基准；缺乏有效的运动补偿技术。

Method: 提出 Event-IMU Stabilized HAR (EIS-HAR)，包含两个模块：(1) EIS 模块，利用非线性扭曲函数重建运动补偿输入以减少运动模糊；(2) HAR 模块，四阶段混合架构高效提取时空特征进行动作识别。同时构建了 DarkShake-DVS 数据集。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明 EIS-HAR 在性能上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 事件相机结合 IMU 能有效解决低光照和剧烈运动下的 HAR 难题，所提出的运动补偿和混合架构设计具备显著优势。

Abstract: Human Action Recognition (HAR) is a fundamental computer vision task with diverse real-world applications. Practical deployments often involve low-light environments and unconstrained 6-DoF camera motion, conditions that degrade visual quality, disrupt temporal coherence, and compromise reliability of existing methods. Event cameras, with high low-light sensitivity and microsecond-level temporal resolution, paired with an inertial measurement unit (IMU), present a promising solution. However, current research faces two key challenges: absence of a benchmark integrating low-light conditions, 6-DoF motion, and synchronized IMU data; and lack of effective motion compensation techniques. To address these, we propose Event-IMU Stabilized HAR (EIS-HAR), with two modules. The first is an EIS module that reduces motion blur via a non-linear warping function to reconstruct a motion-compensated input. The second is a HAR module with a four-stage hybrid architecture to efficiently extract spatiotemporal features for accurate action recognition. To alleviate data scarcity, we introduce DarkShake-DVS, the first large-scale event-based HAR benchmark that includes 18,041 realworld clips captured in low light and intense 6-DoF motion, supplemented by synchronized IMU data. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate consistent superiority of EIS-HAR over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [49] [IndusAgent: Reinforcing Open-Vocabulary Industrial Anomaly Detection with Agentic Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20682)
*Rongbin Tan,Fangfang Lin,Zhenlong Yuan,Min Qiu,Kejin Cui,Mengmeng Wang,Yi Wang,Zijian Song,Zhiyuan Wang,Jiyuan Wang,Yue Wang,Shuhan Song§,Huawei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IndusAgent，一个工具增强的智能体框架，用于开放词汇工业异常检测。通过构建Indus-CoT结构化数据集、动态编排外部工具（区域裁剪、高频增强、先验检索）以及门控强化学习目标，在五个工业异常检测基准上实现了最先进的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在开放词汇工业异常检测中受限于领域不匹配的推理和幻觉性结构推断，需要一种能够主动解决视觉模糊性并解耦细微异常的方法。

Method: 1) 构建Indus-CoT结构化数据集，整合全局视觉观察、高分辨率局部块和专家正常先验；2) 提出IndusAgent动态编排外部工具（动态区域裁剪、高频特征增强、先验检索）；3) 引入门控强化学习目标，联合优化异常分类、定位精度、异常类型推理和高效工具使用。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA、MPDD、DTD和SDD五个工业异常检测基准上，IndusAgent在所有现有方法中取得了最先进的零样本性能。

Conclusion: IndusAgent通过工具增强的智能体框架有效解决了开放词汇工业异常检测中的领域不匹配和幻觉问题，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown remarkable capability in bridging visual perception and textual reasoning, enabling zero-shot understanding across diverse industrial scenarios. However, their performance in open-vocabulary industrial anomaly detection (IAD) is often limited by domain-misaligned reasoning and hallucinated structural inferences. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{IndusAgent}, a tool-augmented agentic framework for open-vocabulary IAD. Specifically, we first construct \textbf{Indus-CoT}, a structured dataset that integrates global visual observations, high-resolution local patches, and expert normalcy priors, providing supervision for fine-tuning the model on rigorous industrial inspection trajectories. Building on this, IndusAgent dynamically orchestrates a set of external tools, including dynamic region cropping, high-frequency feature enhancement, and prior retrieval, thus enabling the agent to actively resolve visual ambiguities and disentangle subtle anomalies. Furthermore, we introduce a gated reinforcement learning objective that jointly optimizes anomaly classification, localization accuracy, anomaly type reasoning, and efficient tool usage, ensuring that tool invocation occurs only when beneficial. Extensive evaluations on five industrial anomaly benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, VisA, MPDD, DTD, and SDD, demonstrate that IndusAgent achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance among all existing methods, validating our robustness and generalization capacity.

</details>


### [50] [Rethinking Cross-Layer Information Routing in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20708)
*Chao Xu,Maohua Li,Qirui Li,Yixuan Xu,Yanke Zhou,Yunhe Li,Cuifeng Shen,Hanlin Tang,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Lin Qu,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffusion-Adaptive Routing (DAR)，一种可学习、时间步自适应、非增量的残差替换方案，用于改善扩散Transformer中跨层信息流的问题，在ImageNet 256×256上使SiT-XL/2的FID从9.67提升至7.56，并实现8.75×训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)在各个设计维度（分词、注意力、条件控制、目标函数、潜变量自编码器）上都经过了大量改进，但残差流（residual stream）——即信息如何在各层之间累积传递——却直接沿用了原始Transformer的设计。作者通过系统分析发现传统残差连接存在三个问题：前向幅度单调膨胀、反向梯度严重衰减、以及显著的块级冗余，因此需要更优的跨层信息路由机制。

Method: 提出Diffusion-Adaptive Routing (DAR)，作为一种可替换残差连接的drop-in方案。DAR通过学习的方式、根据去噪时间步自适应地、以非增量方式聚合历史子层输出，从而替代传统的残差加法。该方法兼容现有增强方法如REPA。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，DAR将SiT-XL/2的FID从9.67提升至7.56（提升2.11），且仅需8.75×更少的训练迭代即可达到基线收敛质量。叠加在REPA上，早期阶段可实现2×训练加速。此外，DAR可应用于大规模T2I模型的微调阶段，在分布匹配蒸馏过程中保留高频细节。

Conclusion: 跨层信息路由是扩散建模中一个未被充分探索的设计维度，其与现有的表示对齐目标函数正交。DAR证明了改进残差流可以有效提升生成质量和训练效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a de facto backbone of modern visual generation, and nearly every major axis of their design -- tokenization, attention, conditioning, objectives, and latent autoencoders -- has been extensively revisited. The residual stream that governs how information accumulates across layers, however, has been directly inherited from the original Transformer. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical analysis of cross-layer information flow in DiTs, jointly along depth and denoising timestep, and identify three concrete symptoms of traditional residual addition, namely monotonic forward magnitude inflation, sharp backward gradient decay, and pronounced block-wise redundancy. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Diffusion-Adaptive Routing (\textsc{DAR}), a drop-in residual replacement that performs \emph{learnable, timestep-adaptive, and non-incremental} aggregation over the history of sublayer outputs. Moreover, the proposed \textsc{DAR} is compatible with many modern Transformer enhancement methods, such as REPA. On ImageNet $256\times256$, \textsc{DAR} improves SiT-XL/2 by $2.11$ FID ($7.56$ vs.\ $9.67$) and matches the baseline's converged quality with $8.75\times$ fewer training iterations. Stacked on top of REPA, it yields a $2\times$ training acceleration in the early stage, suggesting cross-layer information routing as an underexplored design axis in diffusion modeling, one that operates orthogonally to existing representation-alignment objectives. Beyond pretraining, \textsc{DAR} can also be applied during the fine-tuning stage of large-scale T2I models and preserves high-frequency details during Distribution Matching Distillation.

</details>


### [51] [SAVER: Selective As-Needed Vision Evidence for Multimodal Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20713)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Bokun Wang,Rui Chen,Xin Wang,Xiaobo Guo,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVER提出了一种选择性视觉融合框架，通过Conformal Groundability Gate判断是否需要视觉信息，并利用子模选择器从多图中提取紧凑证据，在MNER和MRE任务上超越全时多模态基线，同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中的多模态信息抽取面临多图与文本弱相关、冗余甚至误导的问题，全时多模态融合浪费计算资源且可能放大虚假视觉线索，核心挑战在于决定何时需要视觉信息以及选择哪些图像作为可靠证据。

Method: 提出SAVER框架，包含：1) Conformal Groundability Gate (CGG) 基于保形预测和Clopper-Pearson上界校准激活阈值，在MNER中估计跨度级视觉可接地性，在MRE中推导成对级激活；2) 子模相关-多样性选择器跨图像选择紧凑证据子集；3) Set Transformer聚合所选证据；4) 能量启发的联合评分头融合文本、可选视觉证据、图文一致性和稀疏路由进行实体类型分类或关系分类。

Result: SAVER在MNER和MRE任务上持续优于强文本基线及全时多模态基线，F1提升，AURC降低，在固定风险水平下激活覆盖率增加，同时降低FLOPs和P90延迟。

Conclusion: 按需选择性视觉融合是处理社交媒体多图弱相关场景的有效范式，既提升性能又降低计算开销。

Abstract: Multimodal IE in social media is difficult because a post may attach multiple images that are weakly related, redundant, or even misleading with respect to the text. In this setting, always-on multimodal fusion wastes computation and can amplify spurious visual cues. The core challenge is to decide, for each candidate span or marked entity pair, whether vision should be consulted at all and, if so, which small subset of images provides trustworthy evidence.
  We propose SAVER, a selective vision-as-needed framework for multimodal named entity recognition and multimodal relation extraction. SAVER uses a Conformal Groundability Gate (CGG) to estimate span-level visual groundability in MNER, derive pair-level activation in MRE from the two marked entities, and calibrate the activation threshold on a held-out split via a conformal-style procedure with Clopper--Pearson upper bounds. When activated, a submodular relevance--diversity selector chooses a compact evidence subset across images, which is then aggregated by a Set Transformer. An energy-inspired joint scoring head combines text, optional visual evidence, text--image consistency, and sparse routing for entity typing or relation classification.
  Experiments show that SAVER consistently improves F1 over strong text-only and always-on multimodal baselines, while reducing AURC, increasing activation coverage at a fixed risk level, and lowering FLOPs and P90 latency.

</details>


### [52] [Holistic Reliability Propagation: Decoupling Annotation and Prediction for Robust Noisy-Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20725)
*Jingyang Mao,Ningkang Peng,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Holistic Reliability Propagation (HRP) 方法，通过双层级元学习为每个样本估计两个解耦的可靠性标量（alpha 用于给定标签，beta 用于伪标签），并分别路由到不同目标函数中（可靠性感知 Mixup 和 beta 门控的对比学习），在多媒体分类的噪声标签场景下取得了优于强基线的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将外部标注和模型预测合并为单一可靠性权重，忽略了两种来源可能因不同原因失效的问题。本文旨在解耦两种可靠性，使它们能够被独立估计并路由到不同目标，以更鲁棒地处理噪声标签。

Method: 1) 使用双层级元学习为每个样本估计两个解耦的可靠性标量 alpha（给定标签的可靠性）和 beta（伪标签的可靠性），通过批归一化标量输出且不约束二者之和为1。2) Holistic Reliability Propagation (HRP) 将这两个标量路由到不同目标：在输入分支使用可靠性感知的 Mixup 并施加全局门控；在对比分支使用 beta 门控的伪标签正样本。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的噪声标签基准上，HRP 的平均准确率优于强基线方法，并且在最高噪声率下仍保持竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 解耦两种可靠性并分别路由到不同目标函数是一种有效的噪声标签处理策略，尤其在多媒体分类任务中，该方法能在极端噪声条件下保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning with noisy labels in multimedia classification often combines external annotations and model predictions into a single reliability weight, even though the two sources can fail for different reasons. We instead estimate disentangled reliabilities: bilevel meta-learning produces two batch-normalized scalars per sample, alpha for the given label and beta for the pseudo-label, without constraining them to sum to one. Holistic Reliability Propagation (HRP) then routes them to different objectives, using reliability-aware Mixup with global gating on the input branch and beta-gated pseudo-label positives on the contrastive branch. On synthetic and real-world benchmarks, HRP improves average accuracy over strong baselines and remains competitive at the highest noise rates.

</details>


### [53] [GAMR: Geometric-Aware Manifold Regularization with Virtual Outlier Synthesis for Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20727)
*Ningkang Peng,Jingyang Mao,Xiaoqian Peng,Peirong Ma,Xichen Yang,Weiguang Qu,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知流形正则化范式，通过主动合成虚拟异常样本来构建数据流形间的能量边界，增强特征空间中难样本与噪声样本的可区分性，从而提升DNN在噪声标签下的鲁棒性，在多个基准上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于被动筛选干净样本的方法在受噪声退化的特征空间中难以区分难样本与噪声样本，存在性能瓶颈。本文首次强调主动重塑特征空间几何结构对于从噪声数据中学习的重要性。

Method: 提出几何感知流形正则化范式，核心是通过主动合成虚拟异常样本，在数据流形之间显式构建能量边界；施加几何约束促进类内紧凑性和类间分离性，增强难样本与噪声样本的判别能力。

Result: 在CIFAR-10等多个基准上超越当前SOTA方法，尤其在更具挑战性的非对称噪声条件下优势显著；同时显著提升了模型在开放世界场景中的OOD检测能力。

Conclusion: 该方法具有高度通用性，不依赖于任何噪声模式的先验假设，可作为独立机制无缝集成到现有样本选择框架中，提供更强的抗噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) experience significant performance degradation when processing noisy labels, primarily due to overfitting on mislabeled data. Current mainstream approaches attempt to mitigate this issue by passively filtering clean samples during training. However, simple sample filtering within feature spaces degraded by noise struggles to distinguish between challenging samples and noisy samples, creating a bottleneck for model performance. We highlight for the first time the fundamental importance of actively reshaping feature space geometry for learning from noisy data. We propose a novel Geometry-aware Manifold Regularization Paradigm whose core idea is to explicitly construct energy barriers between data manifolds by actively synthesizing virtual outlier samples. By imposing geometric constraints that promote intra-class compactness and inter-class separation, this approach enhances the discriminability between hard and noisy samples, leading to the learning of more robust representations. Our regularization mechanism exhibits high universality, with effectiveness independent of any prior assumptions about noise patterns. It can be integrated as a standalone mechanism into existing sample selection frameworks, providing stronger robustness against diverse noisy environments. Experiments demonstrate that our paradigm achieves performance surpassing current state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods on multiple benchmarks, including CIFAR-10, with particularly pronounced advantages under more challenging asymmetric noise conditions. Furthermore, this paradigm significantly enhances the model's capability in Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection, ensuring superior reliability and safety for deployment in open-world scenarios.

</details>


### [54] [Early High-Frequency Injection for Geometry-Sensitive OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20728)
*Chuanjie Cheng,Ningkang Peng,Chenxi Liu,Yifan He,Peirong Ma,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现低频输入波段导致ID/OOD特征差异弱，高频波段提供更强可分离性，据此提出EIHF方法——在首个卷积层前增强高频输入信号，无需改变训练目标即可重塑类条件特征几何，减少Mahalanobis距离重叠，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上取得良好OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-hoc OOD检测器依赖于训练后表示中已编码的几何结构，但现有方法未深入分析不同频率输入波段对ID/OOD特征可分离性的影响，作者希望通过频带MMD²诊断揭示这一关系。

Method: 提出EIHF（Expose Input High-Frequency），在第一个卷积层之前对输入进行高频增强处理，是一种输入侧干预方法，不改变训练目标；基于band-wise MMD²分析诊断CE、SimCLR、SupCon、PALM等方法的频带特性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上获得性能提升，在ImageNet-100上取得最佳平均FPR95和第二佳平均AUROC；但在场景中心的数据集Places上存在局限性。

Conclusion: 高频波段对OOD检测的几何敏感方法至关重要，EIHF通过输入侧高频增强有效改善特征几何结构，但场景类别的偏移仍构成挑战。

Abstract: Post-hoc OOD detectors score logits or features after training, so their success depends on the geometry already encoded in the representation. We revisit this assumption through a band-wise MMD^2 analysis across CE, SimCLR, SupCon, and the OOD-oriented representation method PALM. In our diagnostic, low-frequency input bands induce weaker ID/OOD feature discrepancy, whereas higher-frequency bands tend to provide stronger separability. This observation motivates EIHF, an input-side intervention that exposes high-frequency evidence before the first convolution without changing the training objective. EIHF is strongest for geometry-sensitive OOD detection: under matched training and scoring settings, it reshapes class-conditional feature geometry and reduces ID/OOD Mahalanobis score overlap. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 show gains on CIFAR-100 and the best average FPR95 with second-best average AUROC on ImageNet-100, while also revealing a limitation on the scene-centric Places shift. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EIHF.

</details>


### [55] [TASTE: A Designer-Annotated Multi-Dimensional Preference Dataset for AI-Generated Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20731)
*Haonan Zhu,Elad Hirsch,Alexandria Minetti,Allison Nulty,Purvanshi Mehta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASTE数据集包含10位专业设计师对4个文本到图像模型在9个标准上的评分，揭示了当前自动评估系统与人类设计师评价之间存在显著差距。该论文提供了用于分析评分一致性的统计框架，并证明一个小型差异头模型可缩小约一半的评估差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像模型评估使用照片风格偏好数据，仅提供单一整体判断，但设计师从排版、视觉层次、色彩和谐、布局和文本忠实度等多个独立维度进行评估，单一标签无法捕捉这些维度信息。

Method: 构建TASTE数据集：10位专业设计师在9个标准上对4个文本到图像模型输出进行评分，每标准1600个评分。提出标准无关的信号测试框架（使用Kendall's tau、多数概率和Condorcet循环）。基准测试包括6个视觉语言模型（3B-33B参数）和3个专用文本到图像评分器。训练小型成对差异头模型。

Result: 设计师在图形设计任务上的一致性介于食物偏好、电影偏好与照片风格图像质量之间，所有TASTE标准均拒绝随机评分者零假设。无预训练系统超过0.55宏观一致率；视觉语言模型在位置偏差和内容敏感性之间权衡，扩展规模不提升准确率。小差异头模型达到0.611，接近0.741的单评分者上限的一半差距。

Conclusion: 当前自动评估系统在图形设计质量评估上与人类设计师存在显著差距，标准无关的信号测试框架能有效检验评分者一致性，小型差异头模型可部分弥合这一差距，但仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Text-to-image models produce graphic design at production scale, but their supervision comes from photo-style preference data with a single overall verdict per comparison. Designers evaluate along several distinct axes, including typography, visual hierarchy, color harmony, layout, and brief fidelity, and a single label collapses them. We release TASTE (Typography, Aesthetics, Spatial, Tone, Etc.): ten professional designers ranked outputs from four current text-to-image models on nine criteria across two disjoint cohorts, yielding 1,600 ratings per criterion plus per-image hallucination flags on the holistic-preference cohorts. We pair the dataset with three contributions. First, a criterion-agnostic signal test framework, using Kendall's tau, majority probability, and Condorcet cycles against exact iid-uniform nulls at p = 4 and R = 5, places designer agreement on graphic design between food and movie preferences and photo-style image quality, with every TASTE criterion rejecting the random-rater null. Second, no pre-trained system in our benchmark, including six open-weight VLM judges from 3B to 33B parameters and three dedicated T2I scorers, HPSv2.1, PickScore-v1, and LAION-Aesthetic-V2, exceeds 0.55 macro agreement with the 5-designer majority; VLM judges trade off position bias against content sensitivity, so scaling moves along this frontier without improving accuracy. Third, a small pairwise-difference head trained on TASTE reaches 0.611, closing roughly half the gap to the 0.741 single-rater ceiling.

</details>


### [56] [Deep Attention Reweighting: Post-Hoc Attention-Based Feature Aggregation in CNNs for Disentangling Core and Spurious Features under Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20732)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DFR由于依赖GAP导致特征纠缠，限制了其去除虚假相关的能力；本文提出DAR，用可训练的注意力聚合模块替换GAP，在空间维度上自适应加权特征图，从而在特征坍塌前抑制虚假特征，显著优于DFR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN常利用数据集中的虚假相关性学习表面预测性但因果无关的特征，导致泛化性和公平性问题。DFR作为事后矫正方法，受限于纠缠特征，无法同时放大核心特征和抑制虚假特征。

Method: 提出Deep Attention Reweighting (DAR)，一种基于注意力的事后聚合模块，替换GAP层，与分类头联合重训练。DAR对特征图的空间位置进行自适应加权，在特征坍塌为纠缠表示之前选择性抑制虚假特征。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种指标和消融实验中，DAR consistently outperforms DFR，证明基于注意力的聚合能减轻GAP导致的特征纠缠，降低对虚假相关性的依赖。

Conclusion: GAP层是导致特征纠缠的关键瓶颈，用可训练注意力聚合替代GAP能有效解耦核心与虚假特征，提升模型泛化性和公平性。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often exploit spurious correlations in datasets, learning superficially predictive yet causally irrelevant features, leading to poor generalization and fairness issues. Deep Feature Reweighting (DFR) is a post-hoc technique that reduces a trained model's reliance on spurious correlations by retraining its classification head on a target dataset. However, we show that DFR is fundamentally constrained by operating on entangled features, limiting its ability to amplify the core features while simultaneously suppressing the spurious ones. We trace this entanglement to the ubiquitous Global Average Pooling (GAP) layer, which indiscriminately collapses spatially distinct core and spurious features into a single representation. To address this, we propose Deep Attention Reweighting (DAR), a post-hoc attention-based aggregation module that replaces GAP and is retrained jointly with the classification head. DAR computes an adaptive weighting of spatial locations across feature maps, enabling selective suppression of spurious features before the collapse into entangled features. Across various datasets, metrics, and ablations, DAR consistently outperforms DFR, demonstrating that our attention-based aggregation mitigates GAP-induced entanglement and reduces spurious reliance.

</details>


### [57] [Sketch2MinSurf: Vision-Language Guided Generation of Editable Minimal Surfaces from Hand-Drawn Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20733)
*Wenda Wang,Anqi Liu,Junqi Yang,Lei He,Luying Wang,Jiachen Lu,Weixin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch2MinSurf是一个混合视觉-语言与几何优化框架，结合视觉语言引导和极小曲面理论，从手绘草图生成可直接编辑的光滑3D曲面，在100张草图测试集上拓扑相似度达0.844。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（GAN、NeRF、扩散模型）难以从手绘草图生成非欧几里得曲面且保持拓扑一致性，无法产生可直接用于下游设计工作流的可编辑流形。

Method: 提出空间-拓扑编码（将几何表示为节点坐标与实/虚边骨架的元组），引入Sketch2MinSurf结构损失（S2MS-Loss）作为奖励调制目标，联合约束几何重建与拓扑一致性，融合视觉-语言引导与极小曲面理论。

Result: 在100张草图测试集上拓扑相似度达0.844，优于现有草图到形状基线，生成的流形可直接编辑且无伪流形伪影，在大学公共艺术装置中展示了应用潜力。

Conclusion: Sketch2MinSurf有效实现了从手绘草图到可编辑3D曲面的生成，证明了视觉-语言引导与极小曲面理论结合的可行性，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Converting hand-drawn sketches into structured 3D geometries remains challenging due to the difficulty of representing non-Euclidean surfaces and maintaining topological consistency. Existing generative models such as GANs, NeRFs, and diffusion architectures often fail to produce editable manifolds directly usable in downstream design workflows. We present Sketch2MinSurf, a hybrid vision-language and geometric optimization framework that integrates vision-language guidance with minimal-surface theory to generate smooth and editable 3D surfaces from hand-drawn sketches. The core of our approach is a spatial-topological encoding that represents geometry as tuples of node coordinates and real/virtual edge skeletons, enabling stable topological control during generation. We further introduce the Sketch2MinSurf Structural Loss (S2MS-Loss), a reward-modulated objective that jointly constrains geometric reconstruction and topological coherence. On a test set of 100 sketches, Sketch2MinSurf achieves a topological similarity score of 0.844, outperforming existing sketch-to-shape baselines. The generated manifolds are directly editable and free from non-manifold artifacts. A public art installation at a university showcases the method's potential for human-intent-driven 3D form generation. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Sketch2MinSurf/.

</details>


### [58] [Resolving Long-Tail Ambiguity in Unsupervised 3D Point Cloud Segmentation with Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20737)
*Siqi Wei,Hongbin Xu,Feng Xiao,Tian Lan,Chun Li,Ming Li,Qiuxia Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LangTail框架，利用语言模型中的均衡世界知识作为语义先验，通过层次化聚类与对比对齐，解决无监督3D点云分割中长尾模糊性导致的小类别特征被主导簇吸收的问题，在三个数据集上分别提升+13.5、+12.9、+8.9 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督3D点云分割方法依赖纯视觉相似性的聚类范式，存在长尾模糊性（long-tail ambiguity）的根本缺陷：小类别特征持续被主导簇吸收，导致预测严重不平衡。

Method: 提出LangTail，一种语言引导的层次化学习框架。首先从语言模型中提取实体级语义先验，获得跨类别均衡且细粒度的世界知识；然后通过对比对齐将这些先验注入层次化聚类框架，引导多粒度语义结构形成，防止小类别被主导簇吸收，从而为欠表征类别生成更具判别性的表示。

Result: 在ScanNet-v2、S3DIS和nuScenes上分别比现有方法提升+13.5、+12.9、+8.9 mIoU，显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 语言先验能有效改善3D点云中少数类别的表征，缓解无监督分割中的长尾偏差问题。

Abstract: Existing approaches for unsupervised 3D point cloud segmentation predominantly rely on a purely visual similarity-based learning-by-clustering paradigm, which suffers from a fundamental limitation: long-tail ambiguity. In such a paradigm, features of minor classes are consistently absorbed by dominant clusters, leading to severely imbalanced predictions. To address this issue, we propose LangTail, a language-guided hierarchical learning framework that leverages the balanced world knowledge encoded in language models to mitigate long-tail ambiguity in unsupervised 3D segmentation. The key idea is to establish multi-level associations between language-derived semantic priors and visually underrepresented minor classes, thereby compensating for the biased attention of purely visual clustering toward dominant classes. Specifically, LangTail first constructs an entity-level semantic prior from language models, capturing balanced and fine-grained world knowledge across categories. These priors are injected into a hierarchical clustering framework via contrastive alignment. This guides multi-granularity semantic structure formation and prevents minor classes from being absorbed by dominant clusters, yielding more discriminative representations for underrepresented categories. Extensive experiments on ScanNet-v2, S3DIS, and nuScenes demonstrate that LangTail consistently outperforms existing methods by significant margins, \ie, +13.5, +12.9, and +8.9 mIoU, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of language priors in improving the representation of minority classes in 3D point clouds. The code will be released at: https://github.com/Whisky0129/langtail_official.

</details>


### [59] [STAR-IOD: Scale-decoupled Topology Alignment with Pseudo-label Refinement for Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20738)
*Yaoteng Zhang,Qing Zhou,Junyu Gao,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-IOD框架，通过子空间解耦拓扑蒸馏（STD）和聚类驱动伪标签生成器（CPG）解决遥感增量目标检测中的类内尺度变化和标注缺失问题，在DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD数据集上分别超越SOTA方法1.7%和2.1% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感影像以连续数据流形式到达，传统检测器在学习新类别时会遗忘旧类别。现有遥感增量目标检测方法忽视了遥感场景中普遍存在的类内尺度变化，削弱了知识迁移和旧知识保留的效果；同时存在标注缺失问题，导致模型将旧类实例误分类为背景。

Method: 提出STAR-IOD框架，包含两个核心模块：1）子空间解耦拓扑蒸馏（STD）模块，通过显式对齐类间拓扑关系并缓解尺度变化引起的类内表征差异，实现结构知识迁移；2）聚类驱动伪标签生成器（CPG），利用K-Means聚类动态识别类别特定阈值，准确区分真阳性目标和背景噪声，缓解旧类标注缺失问题。同时构建了DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD两个遥感增量目标检测数据集。

Result: 在DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD数据集上，STAR-IOD分别以1.7%和2.1% mAP的优势超越现有最先进方法，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，在基类和新类上均保持了较强的检测性能。

Conclusion: STAR-IOD通过拓扑蒸馏和伪标签生成有效解决了遥感增量检测中的类内尺度变化和标注缺失两大挑战，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery typically arrives in the form of continuous data streams. Traditional detectors often forget previously learned categories when learning new ones; therefore, research on Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection (RS-IOD) is of great significance. However, existing methods largely overlook the intra-class scale variations prevalent in remote sensing scenes, which undermines the effectiveness of knowledge transfer and old knowledge preservation. Moreover, RS-IOD also suffers from missing annotations, which cause the model to misclassify old-class instances as background. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework, STAR-IOD. First, we introduce a Subspace-decoupled Topology Distillation (STD) module to transfer structural knowledge, explicitly aligning inter-class topological relationships and mitigating intra-class representation discrepancies induced by scale shifts. Furthermore, we introduce the Clustering-driven Pseudo-label Generator (CPG), a plug-and-play module that leverages K-Means clustering to dynamically identify class-specific thresholds, thereby guaranteeing an accurate distinction between true positive targets and background noise and alleviating the issue of missing annotations for old classes. We also constructed two Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection datasets, DIOR-IOD and DOTA-IOD to facilitate research on RS-IOD. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches by 1.7% and 2.1% mAP on DIOR-IOD and DOTA-IOD, respectively, effectively alleviating catastrophic forgetting while preserving strong detection performance on both base and novel classes. The code and dataset are released at: https://github.com/zyt95579/STAR-IOD.

</details>


### [60] [SpineContextResUNet: A Computationally Efficient Residual UNet for Spine CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20760)
*K S Nithurshen,Saurabh J. Shigwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Automated segmentation of the vertebral column in Computed Tomography (CT) scans is a prerequisite for pathological assessment and surgical planning. However, state-of-the-art methods, particularly those based on Transformers or large-scale ensembles, demand substantial GPU resources, creating a barrier for clinical adoption in resource-constrained environments or on edge devices. To address this, we introduce SpineContextResUNet, a computationally efficient 3D Residual U-Net designed for rapid spinal localization. Our architecture integrates a lightweight Context Block that employs parallel multi-dilated convolutions to capture long-range anatomical dependencies without the high latency of Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) or the memory overhead of Self-Attention mechanisms. Extensive validation on two public benchmarks, VerSe2020 and CTSpine1K, demonstrates that our model achieves a Dice score of 88.17% and 88.13% respectively. To evaluate performance under strict hardware constraints, we compared our model against a bottlenecked SwinUNETR scaled to match our ~1.7M hardware footprint. While the constrained Transformer suffers severe performance degradation due to a lack of spatial inductive biases in a limited-data regime, our CNN-based approach successfully maintains high accuracy. Crucially, heavy baselines like TotalSegmentator fail due to memory exhaustion on commodity hardware (Intel Core i5, 8GB RAM), our model performs robust inference, making it a viable solution for point-of-care diagnostics and deployment on edge platforms like the Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano.

</details>


### [61] [Diffuse to Detect: Bi-Level Sample Rebalancing with Pseudo-Label Diffusion for Point-Supervised Infrared Small-Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20766)
*Zhu Liu,Yuanhang Yao,Ping Qian,Zihang Chen,Risheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的扩散标注框架，通过热辐射与热扩散的内在一致性，将单点标注扩展为可靠伪掩码，并结合双层双重更新机制联合优化检测器权重、样本权重和扩散参数，以解决红外小目标检测中点监督中伪标签不稳定和样本分布不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测中，点监督方法面临两个耦合瓶颈：在杂波、低对比度红外图像中伪标签演化不稳定，以及样本分布严重不平衡，限制了检测性能。

Method: 1）提出物理诱导的标注策略，利用热辐射模式与热扩散的内在一致性，将单点标签扩展为可靠的伪掩码；2）提出双层双重更新框架，联合优化检测器权重、样本权重和扩散参数；3）元分类器动态预测样本级损失权重；4）可微扩散模块利用检测反馈细化伪标签，实现训练与超参数优化的自适应交互。

Result: 在多个数据集上实现五倍标注加速、更优的检测精度，仅用30%训练数据即可达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了物理引导扩散标注框架在红外小目标检测中的高效性和实用性，显著降低了标注成本同时提升检测性能。

Abstract: Point supervision has become a scalable solution to address dense annotation for infrared small target detection, but its performance is limited by two coupled bottlenecks: unstable pseudo-label evolution in cluttered, low-contrast infrared imagery and severe sample-distribution imbalance. In this paper, we present a more adaptive and stable framework to address these issues. Leveraging the intrinsic consistency between thermal radiation patterns and heat diffusion, we propose a physics-induced annotation strategy that expands single-point labels into reliable pseudo-masks. To further enhance supervision and alleviate sample imbalance, we develop a bi-level dual-update framework that jointly optimizes detector weights, sample weights, and diffusion parameters. A meta-classifier dynamically predicts sample-wise loss weights, while a differentiable diffusion module refines pseudo-labels with detection feedback, enabling adaptive interaction between training and hyperparameter optimization. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate five-fold annotation acceleration, superior detection accuracy, and comparable performance with 30% of the training data, validating the efficiency and practicality of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/yuanhang-yao/diffuse-to-detect.

</details>


### [62] [VIHD: Visual Intervention-based Hallucination Detection for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20772)
*Jiayi Chen,Benteng Ma,Zehui Liao,Winston Chong,Yasmeen George,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIHD方法，通过视觉标记掩码校准语义熵，用于检测医学多模态大语言模型中的幻觉现象，在三个医疗VQA基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法多采用外部启发式扰动，忽视了生成token与视觉token之间在解码过程中的内部跨模态依赖关系，导致检测效果不佳。

Method: VIHD包含三步：1) 通过视觉依赖探测(VDP)定位视觉主导的解码层；2) 通过视觉干预解码(VID)对标记进行掩码以校准语义分布；3) 量化校准语义熵(CSE)作为可靠的幻觉信号。

Result: 在三个医学VQA基准和两个医学MLLM上的实验表明，VIHD持续优于现有最先进方法，验证了细粒度视觉依赖对幻觉检测的重要性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的跨模态视觉依赖信息对于提升医学MLLM幻觉检测效果至关重要，VIHD通过内部视觉干预有效地解决了这一问题。

Abstract: While medical Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promise in assisting diagnosis, they still frequently generate hallucinated responses that appear linguistically plausible but lack visual evidence. Such hallucinations pose risks to clinical decision-making and necessitate effective detection. Existing introspective detection methods primarily perform uncertainty estimation or logical verification by analyzing model responses conditioned on original or perturbed inputs. However, such external perturbations are often heuristic and context-agnostic, which overlooks the internal cross-modal dependency between generated tokens and related visual tokens during decoding. To address this issue, we propose VIHD, a Visual Intervention-based Hallucination Detection method that leverages targeted visual token masking to calibrate semantic entropy for more effective hallucination detection. VIHD locates visually dominant decoder layers via Visual Dependency Probing (VDP), executes Visual Intervention Decoding (VID) via token masking to calibrate the semantic distribution, and quantifies the resulting Calibrated Semantic Entropy (CSE) as a reliable hallucination signal. Extensive experiments on three medical VQA benchmarks with two medical MLLMs demonstrate that VIHD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, underscoring the importance of fine-grained visual dependency for hallucination detection. The code will be available at https://github.com/Jiayi-Chen-AU/VIHD

</details>


### [63] [AttriStory: Fine-grained Attribute Realization for Visual Storytelling with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20777)
*Manogna Sreenivas,Rohit Kumar,Soma Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AttriStory是一个面向视觉故事生成中属性实现（attribute realization）的基准和方法。它包含200个多场景故事（10种艺术风格），并提出了一种即插即用的潜在优化模块AttriLoss，通过早期去噪步的交叉注意力图对齐，确保细粒度属性（如服装颜色、纹理）在生成图像中准确呈现，与现有角色一致性方法正交兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型驱动的视觉故事生成能保持角色跨场景一致性，但缺乏系统方法确保细粒度属性（如服装颜色、纹理、配饰）在生成图像中被忠实渲染。这构成了角色一致性与属性实现之间的关键鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构建AttriStory基准：通过LLM生成200个多场景故事（10种艺术风格），每个场景带有详细属性规格。2) 提出即插即用的潜在优化模块：在早期去噪步（模型建立结构与语义内容时）通过AttriLoss目标函数运作，最大化期望属性-对象对的交叉注意力图对齐，同时抑制虚假关联，指导模型正确定位属性。该方法与现有一致性机制正交，无需架构修改。

Result: 在所有基线上，引入AttriLoss均取得一致的改进效果，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 本文将属性实现定位为视觉故事生成中一个与角色一致性互补的独立维度，推动了领域向细粒度属性可控故事生成发展。

Abstract: Visual storytelling with diffusion models has made impressive strides in maintaining character consistency across narrative scenes. However, a critical gap remains: while these methods ensure a character remains consistent across scenes, they provide no systematic method to ensure if fine-grained attributes such as color and textures of clothing, accessories are faithfully rendered in the generated images. Towards this goal, we introduce AttriStory, a benchmark enabling attribute realization in visual storytelling. We curate 200 multi-scene stories across 10 distinct artistic styles using Large Language Model. Each scene is constructed with detailed attribute specifications to enable rich visual narratives. Further, to address attribute realization, we propose a plug-and-play latent optimization module that operates during early denoising steps, when the model establishes structural and semantic content. We achieve this through AttriLoss objective designed to maximize alignment between the cross-attention maps for desired attribute-object pairs while suppressing spurious associations, guiding models to localize attributes correctly. This approach operates orthogonally to existing consistency mechanisms, integrating seamlessly with current story generation pipelines without requiring architectural modifications. Our experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on incorporating AttriLoss across all baselines. This work positions attribute realization as a distinct, complementary dimension of visual storytelling, alongside character consistency, advancing the field toward fine-grained attribute-controlled story generation. Project-page:https://manogna-s.github.io/attristory/

</details>


### [64] [Findings of the Counter Turing Test: AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20787)
*Rajarshi Roy,Nasrin Imanpour,Ashhar Aziz,Shashwat Bajpai,Gurpreet Singh,Shwetangshu Biswas,Kapil Wanaskar,Parth Patwa,Subhankar Ghosh,Shreyas Dixit,Nilesh Ranjan Pal,Vipula Rawte,Ritvik Garimella,Amitava Das,Amit Sheth,Vasu Sharma,Aishwarya Naresh Reganti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Defactify 4.0研讨会提出的反图灵测试（CT2）任务，用于检测AI生成图像。通过构建包含50,000张合成图像的MS COCOAI数据集，参与者采用CNN、ViT、频率分析等多种方法进行二分类和生成模型识别。结果表明AI图像检测可达高准确率（F1>0.83），但模型溯源仍极具挑战（最高F1=0.4986）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（如Stable Diffusion、DALL-E、Midjourney）的快速发展带来了虚假信息、偏见内容等严重挑战，日益逼真的AI生成图像使得检测成为研究人员、政策制定者和行业利益相关者的迫切关注。

Method: 构建MS COCOAI数据集（50,000张来自多种生成模型的合成图像+MS COCO真实图像），组织Counter Turing Test（CT2）竞赛，设置两个任务：二分类（AI生成vs真实）和生成模型溯源。参与者采用CNN、Vision Transformer、频率分析、对比学习、多模态等多种检测策略。

Result: AI图像二分类检测准确率较高（F1>0.83），但特定生成模型识别准确率较低（最高F1=0.4986）。

Conclusion: 现有方法虽能有效区分AI图像与真实图像，但模型指纹识别能力不足，亟需改进模型溯源、对抗鲁棒性和实时检测机制。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in generative AI technologies, such as Stable Diffusion, DALL-E, and Midjourney, have significantly transformed the creation of synthetic visual content. While these models enable innovation across industries, they also pose serious challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, and biased content generation. The increasing realism of AI-generated images makes their detection a pressing concern for researchers, policymakers, and industry stakeholders.
  In this paper, we present the findings of the Defactify 4.0 workshop, which introduced the Counter Turing Test (CT2) for AI-Generated Image Detection. The competition consisted of two key tasks: (1) binary classification of images as either AI-generated or real and (2) identification of the specific generative model responsible for an AI-generated image. To facilitate this, we developed the MS COCOAI dataset, consisting of 50,000 synthetic images from multiple generative models alongside real-world images from the MS COCO dataset.
  Participants employed diverse detection strategies, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), frequency-based analysis, contrastive learning, and multimodal techniques. The results demonstrated that while AI-generated images can be detected with high accuracy (F1-score > 0.83), identifying the exact model used remains significantly more challenging (highest F1-score: 0.4986). These findings highlight the need for improved model fingerprinting, adversarial robustness, and real-time detection mechanisms.

</details>


### [65] [What Semantics Survive the Connector? Diagnosing VLM-to-DiT Alignment in Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20795)
*Hangyu Lin,Chao Wen,Chengming Xu,Jianxiong Gao,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建诊断数据集TRACE-Edit和系统化诊断协议，揭示了流匹配视频生成模型中VLM到DiT的对齐过程会造成细粒度结构语义的严重退化，推翻了对齐无损语义传递的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配视频生成模型依赖预置VLM进行指令式视频编辑，假设连接器模块能无缝对齐VLM的多模态推理与DiT的文本嵌入空间，但这一对齐可能导致细粒度结构变量退化。由于端到端评估混淆了对齐失败与生成错误，且自然数据集缺乏解耦标注，难以验证该假设。

Method: 提出基于视频合成的受控数据处理流程，构建诊断数据集TRACE-Edit（聚焦关系型编辑）；设计全面的诊断协议，分析现有视频编辑模型中元查询（meta-query）和连接器（connector）两个重要设计；对四个代表性模型案例进行系统评估。

Result: 四个代表性模型案例的系统评估显示，细粒度结构语义在对齐过程中会严重退化。

Conclusion: 推翻了VLM到DiT对齐无损语义传递的假设，将对齐过程确定为视频编辑的主要瓶颈，为未来的多模态对齐架构提供了新的诊断基础。

Abstract: Flow matching based video generative models have been increasingly relying on prepended Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to handle complex, instruction-based video editing. The prevailing assumption underlying this paradigm is that a connector module can seamlessly align the VLM's rich multi-modal reasoning with the original text embedding space of DiTs. However, we hypothesize that this alignment acts as a severe semantic bottleneck, degrading fine-grained structural variables. Verifying this is challenging, as end-to-end evaluations conflate alignment failures with generation errors, and natural datasets lack disentangled annotations. To rigorously investigate this, we propose a controlled data processing pipeline based on video composition that results in TRACE-Edit, a diagnostic dataset focusing on relation-based editing. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a comprehensive diagnostic protocol to analyze two important designs of meta-query and connector in the existing video editing models. Systematic evaluation of four representative model cases reveals that fine-grained structural semantics can be severely degraded during alignment. Our findings overturn the assumption of lossless semantic transfer, identifying the VLM-to-DiT alignment as a major bottleneck and providing a new diagnostic foundation for future multi-modal alignment architectures.

</details>


### [66] [OlmoEarth v1.1: A more efficient family of OlmoEarth models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20804)
*Gabriel Tseng,Yawen Zhang,Favyen Bastani,Henry Herzog,Joseph Redmon,Hadrien Sablon,Piper Wolters,Patrick Alan Johnson,Christopher Wilhelm,Patrick Beukema*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了对OlmoEarth系列模型的改进，在保持性能的同时将基础模型训练GPU小时数减少1.7倍，Sentinel-2任务的推理MACs减少2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低OlmoEarth系列模型在训练和推理过程中的计算成本，同时保持模型整体性能。

Method: 提出了一系列对OlmoEarth家族模型的改进方法。

Result: 基础模型训练GPU小时数减少1.7倍，Sentinel-2任务推理MACs减少2.9倍，模型整体性能得以保持。

Conclusion: 这些改进有效降低了训练和推理的计算成本，且不影响模型性能。训练代码已开源。

Abstract: We present a set of improvements to the OlmoEarth family. These improvements allow us to cut compute costs during training ($1.7 \times$ reduction in GPU hours required to train our Base models) and inference ($2.9\times$ reductions in MACs on Sentinel-2 tasks), while maintaining the models' overall performance. All training code is available at github.com/allenai/olmoearth_pretrain.

</details>


### [67] [ArchSIBench: Benchmarking the Architectural Spatial Intelligence of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20837)
*Qirui Shen,Wenda Wang,Jiachen Lu,Zilong Huang,Jin Bai,Lei He,Hongxuan Chen,Weixin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ArchSIBench，一个面向建筑空间智能的基准测试，涵盖感知、推理、导航、变换和配置五个核心维度共17个细粒度子任务，通过3,000个人工标注的问答对评估视觉语言模型（VLM）的建筑空间智能。实验发现多数VLM与人类基线存在显著差距，尤其是空间变换与配置推理方面，部分最先进模型可接近无建筑学训练的人类水平，但与有建筑学训练的人类评估者仍有明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对VLM的空间能力评估仅停留在相对方位、距离比较、物体计数等低级空间认知层面，缺乏对建筑空间高级认知（如布局理解、流线模式、功能分区）的系统评估。为此，作者从建筑学、认知科学和心理学视角出发，构建了专门评估建筑空间智能的基准。

Method: ArchSIBench涵盖感知、推理、导航、变换、配置五个核心维度，包含17个细粒度子任务。由具有建筑学背景的专家手工标注构建3,000个问答对，用于全面评估VLM的建筑空间智能。

Result: 多数VLM与人类基线存在显著差异，不同能力维度上模型表现差异较大。部分最先进模型可接近无建筑学训练的人类水平，但在空间变换和配置推理方面，与受过建筑学训练的人类评估者仍有明显差距。

Conclusion: ArchSIBench为测量和提升VLM的建筑空间智能提供了重要见解和系统资源，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Architectural spatial intelligence, the ability to recognize and infer architectural space, is fundamental to tasks such as robot navigation, embodied interaction, and 3D scene understanding and generation. Although extensive research has evaluated the basic spatial skills of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as relative orientation, distance comparison, and object counting, these tasks cover only the most elementary levels of spatial cognition and largely overlook higher-level cognition of architectural space, including layout understanding, circulation patterns, and functional zoning. In this work, we present ArchSIBench, a Benchmark for Architectural Spatial Intelligence based on the perspectives from architecture, cognitive science, and psychology. ArchSIBench covers five core dimensions: perception, reasoning, navigation, transformation, and configuration, comprising 17 fine-grained subtasks. Through careful manual annotation by experts with architectural backgrounds, we construct 3,000 question-answer pairs to enable comprehensive evaluation of architectural spatial intelligence. Based on ArchSIBench, we evaluate various VLMs and find that the architectural spatial intelligence of most models shows significant differences from human baselines; additionally, models exhibit substantial variability across capability dimensions. Some state-of-the-art models can approach the level of human evaluators without architectural training. However, a clear gap remains compared to human evaluators with architectural training, particularly in spatial transformation and configuration reasoning. We believe that ArchSIBench will provide important insights and systematic resources for measuring and advancing the architectural spatial intelligence of VLMs. The dataset and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ArchSIBench/ArchSIBench.

</details>


### [68] [Decomposing Subject-Driven Image Generation via Intermediate Structural Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20807)
*Hanzhong Guo,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，先预测Canny边缘图再基于源外观和预测结构进行渲染，以在主体驱动文本到图像生成中保留高频身份细节（如标志、图案、文字）；同时构建了10万对文本感知数据集，实验表明该方法优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动文本到图像生成方法直接在RGB空间操作，在较大编辑下难以保留标志、图案、文字等高频率身份细节，导致细节退化。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段预测Canny边缘图，将结构与外观解耦；第二阶段以源外观和预测结构为条件渲染最终图像。此外，构建了10万对文本感知数据集，通过全自动流程保证跨视图文本一致性。

Result: 基于GPT-4.1的评估和知识蒸馏研究显示，该方法在多个基线上取得明显提升，证明了中间结构预测对高保真主体驱动生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 中间结构预测是保持高频身份细节的有效途径，数据集和代码将公开。

Abstract: Subject-driven text-to-image generation still struggles to preserve high-frequency identity details such as logos, patterns, and text. Existing methods typically operate directly in RGB space, which often leads to detail degradation under substantial edits. We propose a two-stage framework that decouples structure from appearance by first predicting a Canny map and then rendering the final image conditioned on both the source appearance and the predicted structure. To improve text handling, we further introduce a fully automatic pipeline that constructs a 100k-pair text-aware dataset with cross-view textual consistency. Experiments, including GPT-4.1-based evaluation and a knowledge distillation study, show clear gains over selected baselines and suggest that intermediate structural prediction is an effective route for high-fidelity subject-driven generation. Our dataset and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [69] [Spatial Gram Alignment for Ultra-High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20808)
*Jinjin Zhang,Xiefan Guo,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间文法对齐（SGA）框架，通过对齐生成特征与基础模型先验的内部自相似性，在不破坏预训练潜扩散模型生成能力的前提下，实现超高分辨率文本到图像合成中全局结构完整性与局部细节保真度的有效平衡，达到当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法在将基础模型（如SAM、DINO）的视觉先验蒸馏到生成潜特征时，扩展至超高分辨率会导致可学习性-保真度冲突——直接的逐块特征蒸馏会扰动预训练潜流形，造成生成质量下降。

Method: 提出Spatial Gram Alignment（SGA）框架，摒弃直接的对齐方式，转而将生成特征与基础模型先验的内部自相似性（self-similarities）进行对齐，施加非侵入式的空间约束。该约束建立宏观结构一致性，同时保留原生LDMs的微观像素级保真度目标。该方法可无缝集成到中间扩散特征和VAE潜变量中。

Result: 大量实验表明，SGA在超高分辨率文本到图像合成任务上达到了当前最优性能，有效协调了全局结构完整性与细粒度视觉细节。

Conclusion: 通过空间自相似性对齐而非直接特征蒸馏，可以解决超高分辨率下LDM的生成退化问题，在保持预训练模型生成能力的同时融入基础模型的先验知识。

Abstract: Modern ultra-high-resolution image synthesis relies heavily on the robust generative capacity of large-scale pre-trained Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs). While recent representation alignment methods have proven effective by distilling visual priors from foundation models (e.g., SAM or DINO) into generative latent features, scaling these approaches to pre-trained LDMs at extreme resolutions exposes a critical learnability-fidelity conflict. Specifically, forcing direct patch-wise feature distillation inherently perturbs the pre-trained latent manifold, ultimately leading to generation degradation. To address this bottleneck, we propose Spatial Gram Alignment (SGA), a novel framework that explicitly leverages the representation priors of vision foundation models while preserving the native generative capacity of LDMs. Moving beyond restrictive direct alignment, SGA imposes a non-invasive spatial constraint by aligning the internal self-similarities of the generative features with those of the foundation priors. This spatial constraint effectively establishes macroscopic structural coherence, while the native generative objectives retain the microscopic pixel-level fidelity inherent to the original LDMs. Notably, this versatile strategy integrates seamlessly across both intermediate diffusion features and VAE latents within pre-trained LDMs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGA achieves state-of-the-art performance for ultra-high-resolution text-to-image synthesis, yielding an effective reconciliation between global structural integrity and fine-grained visual details. Code is available at https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SGA.

</details>


### [70] [OSGNet with MLLM Reranking @ Ego4D Episodic Memory Challenge 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20818)
*Yisen Feng,Leigang Qu,Haoyu Zhang,Qiaohui Chu,Meng Liu,Xuemeng Song,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于重排序框架的解决方案，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对OSGNet生成的候选片段进行精排，在Ego4D情景记忆挑战的自然语言查询和GoalStep两个赛道均获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定位管线效率高、候选召回好，但缺乏细粒度视频-语言推理能力；而MLLM推理能力强但直接处理长视频效率低。如何在保持效率的同时利用MLLM的强推理能力是核心动机。

Method: 先使用现有定位模型OSGNet生成候选时序片段集合，再引入MLLM对这些候选片段与查询的匹配度进行重新排序，选出最佳匹配片段作为最终预测。

Result: 在Ego4D情景记忆挑战的自然语言查询（NLQ）和GoalStep两个赛道均获得第一名。

Conclusion: 重排序框架能够有效兼顾常规定位方法的效率与MLLM的强视频-语言推理能力，在长视频定位任务上取得最优性能。

Abstract: In this report, we present our champion solutions for the Natural Language Queries and GoalStep tracks of the Ego4D Episodic Memory Challenge at CVPR 2026. Both tracks require accurately localizing temporal segments from long untrimmed egocentric videos. To address these tasks, we propose a reranking-based framework that effectively leverages the strong video-language reasoning capability of multimodal large language model (MLLM) while preserving the efficiency and candidate recall of conventional localization pipelines. Specifically, we first obtain a set of candidate segments from existing localization model OSGNet, and then employ MLLM to select the segment that best matches the given query, thereby refining the final prediction. Ultimately, our method achieved first place in both the Natural Language Queries and GoalStep tracks. Our code can be found at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPR25-OSGNet.

</details>


### [71] [VISTA: Technical Report for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20901)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Yisen Feng,Meng Liu,Weili Guan,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个结合V-JEPA时序编码与Faster R-CNN空间检测的StillFast架构，在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D短期物体交互预测挑战中取得了第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Ego4D第一人称视频中短期物体交互预测（STA）任务，即从给定的视频时间戳出发，预测下一次人-物交互的位置、类别、动作、接触时间及置信度。

Method: 采用StillFast双分支设计：1）空间分支使用COCO预训练的Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN从最后一帧高分辨率图像生成物体提议；2）时序分支使用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1提取片段级时序上下文；3）通过特征调制和ROI级上下文融合将时序信息注入检测通路；4）融合后的提议特征送入多头STA预测器进行框精修、名词分类、动词分类、接触时间回归和交互置信度估计；5）最终提交时集成互补预测以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA挑战官方评测中取得第一名。

Conclusion: VISTA验证了结合物体级空间检测与时序上下文（StillFast范式）在短期交互预测任务上的有效性，代码将开源。

Abstract: We propose VISTA, a V-JEPA Integrated StillFast Temporal Anticipator for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation (STA) Challenge at EgoVis 2026. Given an egocentric video timestamp, the task requires anticipating the next human-object interaction, including the future active object's bounding box, noun category, verb category, time-to-contact, and confidence score. VISTA follows a StillFast-style design that combines object-centric spatial detection with short-horizon temporal context. Specifically, a COCO-pretrained Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN detector generates object proposals from the last observed high-resolution frame, while a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 temporal branch extracts clip-level egocentric context from the observed video. The temporal representation is injected into the detection pathway through feature modulation and ROI-level context fusion. The fused proposal features are then passed to multi-head STA predictors for box refinement, noun classification, verb classification, time-to-contact regression, and interaction confidence estimation. For the final submission, we further ensemble complementary predictions to improve robustness. Experimental results on the official challenge server show that VISTA achieves first place in the EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA Challenge. Our code will be released at https://github.com/CorrineQiu/VISTA.

</details>


### [72] [VSCD: Video-based Scene Change Detection in Unaligned Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20821)
*Jiae Yoon,Ue-Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视频场景变化检测（VSCD）任务，给定不同时间、无约束相机运动下的参考视频和查询视频，预测每帧像素级变化掩码。构建了超110万帧标注的大规模基准数据集，并提出查询中心的多参考模型，通过局部块匹配和置信度融合实现SOTA性能，并在移动机器人上验证了实际应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变化检测方法大多假设固定视角、轻微错位或少量变化物体，无法处理无约束相机运动、非时间同步、大量物体出现/消失的真实场景。为推进长期自主机器人的环境感知能力，需要更通用的视频级变化检测设置。

Method: 提出查询中心的多参考模型：1）从变化掩码监督中隐式学习时间匹配；2）通过局部块对应关系对齐候选参考特征到查询帧；3）使用帧级和块级置信度融合每个候选的变化特征；4）每帧解码一次高分辨率掩码。

Result: 在构建的大规模基准上超越基于图像和视频的强基线方法，达到SOTA性能；在真实世界测试集上验证了跨仿真迁移能力；部署到移动机器人上成功应用于视觉监控和物体增量学习两个下游任务。

Conclusion: 视频场景变化检测是可行的且具有实际应用价值，基于查询中心的多参考融合策略有效应对了无约束相机运动下的变化检测挑战，为长期自主机器人的环境感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Detecting what has changed in an environment is essential for long-term autonomy, yet most change detection settings assume fixed viewpoints, mild misalignment, or only a few changed objects. We introduce Video-based Scene Change Detection (VSCD), which predicts a pixel-wise change mask for each query frame, given a reference and a query RGB video of the same indoor space recorded at different times under unconstrained camera motion. The two videos are not temporally synchronized, and many object instances may appear or disappear. To study this setting, we build a large-scale benchmark with over 1.1 million frames annotated with pixel-accurate change masks, together with a real-world test set for evaluating transfer beyond simulation. We propose a query-centric multi-reference model that learns temporal matching implicitly from change-mask supervision, aligns candidate reference features to the query via local patch correspondence, and fuses per-candidate change features using frame-level and patch-level confidence before decoding a high-resolution mask once per frame. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance against strong image- and video-based baselines, and we validate its real-world impact by deploying it on a mobile robot for two downstream applications -- visual surveillance and object incremental learning.

</details>


### [73] [Focus-then-Context: Subject-Centric Progressive Visual Token Reduction for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20950)
*Yulin Zhao,Yun Wang,Dehua Zheng,Borui jiang,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a bottleneck of prohibitive computational costs arising from massive visual token sequences during inference. Existing vision token reduction methods alleviate this burden, but they unintentionally preserve the isolated visual subject strictly aligned with the user's query, which fails to substantially explore salient subjects and their contextual relationships. In this paper, we propose SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction paradigm that emulates the \textit{Focus-then-Context} mechanism of the human visual perception system. Specifically, we first construct a focus identification module to explicitly model the interplay between visual saliency and semantic relevance. Herein, it can excavate the comprehensive visual subject spectrum to ensure a high-fidelity representation of visual input. Subsequently, a context-aware structural scanning module is developed to aggregate contextual cues from neighboring regions. As such, it can effectively restore global relational dependencies to uphold the structural integrity of the preserved subjects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm consistently outperforms SOTA methods, achieving up to 2.53 times speedup with only 22.2% of visual tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA with a negligible 0.6% accuracy drop.

</details>


### [74] [RelWitness: Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation with Visual-Geometric Relation Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20823)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RelWitness提出了一种基于关系见证者的开放词汇3D场景图生成框架，通过视觉-几何线索从不完整的标注中识别可靠的关系，解决关系监督不完整的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图生成面临两大挑战：词汇扩展难度大，以及关系标注不完整——许多有效的对象-谓词关系未被标注，导致监督信号不可靠。

Method: 提出关系见证者（relation witness）概念，即使关系在场景中可观察的具体视觉-几何线索。通过RGB视图、深度图、重建3D几何、角色敏感文本、对象先验空视图和多视角一致性构建见证记录。视觉-几何见证验证器将未标注的关系候选分类为验证的缺失正例、可靠负例或不确定未标案例。采用见证引导的正-无标签目标函数从不完整标注中学习。

Result: 在3DSSG/3RScan和ScanNet开放词汇分割上的模拟实验显示：未见关系识别提升、见证精度更高、幻觉减少、冗余关系短语降低。注：所有数值为规划值，需替换为复现测量结果。

Conclusion: 关系见证者机制能有效利用视觉-几何线索从不完整标注中学习，提升开放词汇3D场景图生成中关系识别的可靠性和精确度。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation seeks to describe object instances and their relations with flexible natural-language predicates. The central difficulty is not only vocabulary expansion, but supervision reliability: relation annotations in 3D scene graph datasets are selective, and many valid object-pair relations are unannotated. We propose RelWitness, a framework for open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation from posed RGB-D sequences under incomplete relation supervision. The key concept is a relation witness: a concrete visual-geometric cue that makes a relation observable in the captured scene. Support relations require contact and vertical ordering; containment requires enclosure; proximity requires metric closeness; orientation requires facing direction; and stable relations should persist across views where both objects are visible. RelWitness constructs relation witness records from RGB views, depth maps, reconstructed 3D geometry, role-sensitive text, object-prior null views, and multi-view consistency. A visual-geometric witness verifier assigns unannotated relation candidates to verified missing positives, reliable negatives, or uncertain unlabeled cases. A witness-guided positive-unlabeled objective then learns from incomplete annotations without turning every missing label into a negative. We further introduce witness-consistent decoding and an RGB-D missing-relation audit protocol. Simulated manuscript-planning experiments on 3DSSG/3RScan and ScanNet-derived open-vocabulary splits show the intended behavior: improved unseen-relation recognition, higher witness precision, lower hallucination, and reduced redundant relation phrases. All numerical results are planning values and must be replaced by reproduced measurements before submission

</details>


### [75] [Finding the Correct Visual Evidence Without Forgetting: Mitigating Hallucination in LVLMs via Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20965)
*Yutong Xie,Zhenglin Hua,Ran Wang,Wing W. Y. Ng,Xizhao Wang,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于层间视觉注意力差异（ILVAD）的无需训练、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法，通过增强视觉证据注意力来减少LVLM在生成过程中的视觉遗忘和幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在生成时容易对视觉证据注意力不足并逐渐遗忘，导致幻觉产生。

Method: 利用早期生成token对视觉token的注意力权重跨层差异，识别重复激活的视觉token形成显著性图；在生成过程中通过显著性图增强对视觉证据的注意力（减少视觉遗忘），同时利用显著性图选择并强调与视觉证据高度关联的文本token。

Result: 在五个最新LVLM上的多项基准评估表明，该方法能一致性地缓解不同架构LVLM的幻觉。

Conclusion: ILVAD是一种有效的无训练幻觉缓解方法，通过利用层间注意力差异增强视觉证据关注度，可即插即用于现有LVLM。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown remarkable performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. Despite this progress, they are still prone to hallucination, generating responses that are inconsistent with visual content. In this work, we find that LVLMs tend to hallucinate when they pay insufficient attention to the correct visual evidence and gradually forget it during the generation process. We empirically find that although LVLMs overall attend insufficiently to visual evidence, they exhibit sensitivity to the correct visual evidence in specific layers, with notable inter-layer discrepancy. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel hallucination mitigation method that enhances visual evidence based on Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy (ILVAD). Specifically, we obtain the attention weights from early generated tokens to visual tokens across layers and identify the tokens that are repeatedly activated as visual evidence, forming a saliency map. We then enhance attention to visual evidence during generation through the saliency map to reduce visual forgetting. In addition, we leverage the saliency map to obtain attention scores of generated text to visual evidence, in order to select and emphasize text tokens that are strongly grounded in visual evidence. Our method is training-free and plug-and-play. Multiple benchmark evaluations conducted on five recently released models show that our method can consistently mitigate hallucinations in different LVLMs over various architectures. Code is available at https://github.com/ytx-ML/ILVAD.

</details>


### [76] [Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Models for Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20971)
*Akhitha Pakala,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Shahzad Memon,Tauseef Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种预训练CNN架构（VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、XceptionNet）在GAN生成图像检测任务中的性能。VGG16以91%准确率最高，其余模型均为90%。EfficientNetB0对假图敏感但真实样本可靠性下降。研究指出数据集不平衡、过拟合和可解释性有限是主要局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAN图像篡改技术日益成熟，对数字取证构成严峻挑战，亟需可靠的假图检测方法。

Method: 使用统一的预处理和训练流程（调整大小、归一化、数据增强），在真实与篡改图像数据集上比较VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet四种预训练CNN架构的性能。

Result: VGG16准确率91%，XceptionNet、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0均为90%。EfficientNetB0对假图敏感度更高但对真实样本可靠性较低。

Conclusion: 研究建立了可复现的基线，强调需要平衡数据集、高级增强策略和公平性感知训练来构建可靠的假图检测系统。

Abstract: The growing sophistication of GAN-based image manipulation presents significant challenges for digital forensics. This study compares the performance of four pretrained CNN architectures including VGG16, ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and XceptionNet for fake image detection using a unified preprocessing and training pipeline. A dataset of real and manipulated images was processed through resizing, normalization, and augmentation to address class imbalance and improve generalization. Models were evaluated using Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. VGG16 achieved the highest accuracy at 91%, with XceptionNet, ResNet50, and EfficientNetB0 each reaching 90%. EfficientNetB0 showed stronger sensitivity to fake images but reduced reliability on real samples, reflecting imbalance-driven bias. Limitations include dataset imbalance, overfitting, and limited interpretability, which affect cross-domain robustness. The study provides a reproducible baseline and underscores the need for balanced datasets, advanced augmentation, and fairness-aware training to develop reliable fake image detection systems.

</details>


### [77] [Hybrid Machine Learning Model for Forest Height Estimation from TanDEM-X and Landsat Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20997)
*Islam Mansour,Ronny Haensch,Irena Hajnsek,Konstantinos Papathanassiou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过融合光学Landsat数据扩展混合模型的特征空间，以改进基于TanDEM-X干涉相干估计森林高度的性能，在加蓬Lopé国家公园的验证中RMSE降低13.5%，MAE降低16.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习与物理模型混合方法在从TanDEM-X数据估计森林高度时，由于特征空间的局限性，无法完全解决高度/结构以及基线/地形坡度间的歧义性问题，亟需引入互补信息来提升物理一致性和估计精度。

Method: 在原有基于TanDEM-X干涉相干测量的混合模型基础上，扩展特征空间，加入光学Landsat数据以提供森林类型或结构等互补信息；在加蓬Lopé国家公园多个TanDEM-X数据集上验证，并以机载LiDAR测量为参考进行精度评估。

Result: 与原始混合模型相比，扩展模型均方根误差（RMSE）降低13.5%，平均绝对误差（MAE）降低16.6%，证实了多光谱输入对森林高度估计的改进效果。

Conclusion: 融合光学多光谱数据能有效解决单一雷达特征空间中的歧义性问题，提升混合模型在森林高度反演中的精度和物理一致性。

Abstract: Integrating machine learning (ML) with physical models (PM) has emerged as a promising way of retrieving geophysical parameters from remote sensing data. In this context, a ML model for estimating forest height from TanDEM-X interferometric coherence measurements has recently been proposed, that constrains the learning process through a PM. While the features used for training and inversion where selected to ensure the physical consistency of the solutions, they could not resolve all height / structure and baseline / terrain slope ambiguities in the data. To improve this, the extension of the feature space with optical Landsat data is proposed able to provide complementary information on forest type or structure. The extended model is applied and validated on several TanDEM-X acquisitions over the Gabonese Lopé national park site and assessed against airborne LiDAR measurements. Results show a 13.5% reduction in RMSE and a 16.6% reduction in MAE compared to the original hybrid model, confirming the added value of multispectral inputs.

</details>


### [78] [Map-Mono-Ego: Map-Grounded Global Human Pose Estimation from Monocular Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20889)
*Hiroyuki Deguchi,Ryosuke Hori,Kotaro Amaya,Tsubasa Maruyama,Mitsunori Tada,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV
